# Documentation Completion Tracker

> The follow-up notes, the phase ledger and each row's notes keep every review's evidence as it
> was recorded. A row's state columns and its source files and tests are current.

This is the authoritative page-by-page state ledger for the English user guide.
It follows the navigation order in `mkdocs.yml`. Historical source paths,
build results, and notes are retained, but they are not current completion
evidence until the row is re-reviewed against the rules in
`docs/dev/USER_GUIDE_AUTHORING.md`.

UG-00 moved all 104 rows conservatively to `Expansion required`. That does not claim prior work was wrong; the earlier single status did not
independently prove current content, source-review, human-review, and media
gates.

## Independent state model

Each row records these gates independently:

- `Content state`: `Inventory`, `Source review`, `Expansion required`,
  `Draft`, `Technical review`, `User review`, or `Content complete`.
- `Media state`: `Not assessed`, `Not needed`, `Planned`, `Captured`, `Placed`,
  `Validated`, or `Deferred`. `Deferred` is outside the current release and is
  not verified media.
- `Source review`: `Not started`, `Mapped`, `In review`,
  `Re-review required`, or `Current`. A current review also requires its
  date, exact revision, dirty reviewed paths, and source/test list.
- `Technical review` and `User review`: each is independent: `Not requested`,
  `Pending`, `Accepted`, or `Findings recorded`, with reviewer, date, and
  cohort in notes or the review packet.
- Reachability, parameter/action, algorithm/behavior, and example/workflow
  coverage are explicit checks, never inferred from content or build status.

A strict build proves structural validity only. It does not advance source,
technical, user, or media state. `Content complete` requires both human reviews
(direct or recorded cohort) and a current strict build. Missing required media
may coexist with `Content complete`, but not with `Validated` media.

## Frozen authoring templates

Required headings/evidence fields may be extended, but not silently removed:

- `templates/PROCESS_PAGE_TEMPLATE.md`
- `templates/COMPONENT_PAGE_TEMPLATE.md`
- `templates/WORKFLOW_PAGE_TEMPLATE.md`
- `templates/REFERENCE_PAGE_TEMPLATE.md`

Verification prerequisites and evidence modes are in
`CONTENT_VERIFICATION_PREREQUISITES.md`.

## Phase ledger procedure

1. Verify preceding phases are `Agent complete`, `Complete`, reasoned
   `Not applicable`, or maintainer-approved `Deferred` with rationale, re-entry
   criteria, and public-page/media disposition. Inspect the worktree and record
   full `HEAD`, dirty reviewed paths, and relevant diffs.
2. Resolve current sources and prerequisites. Change only the selected phase to
   `In progress`; at most one phase may be in progress.
3. Work sequentially. In multi-page phases, finish one review-ready page,
   tracker row, and focused validation before opening the next.
4. Record owner, exact sources/tests, review date/revision/dirty state,
   reachability, control/algorithm/example checks, independent reviews, media,
   and exact strict-build evidence on every affected row.
5. Use `Agent complete` when bounded work and automated gates pass but deferred
   external reviews remain; `Complete` only after all external gates; `Blocked`
   only with exact unavailable prerequisite/completed-work evidence; and
   `Deferred` only after an explicit maintainer release-scope decision. Deferred
   work makes no completion or verification claim.
6. Record changed files, sources/tests, revision/dirty state, decisions,
   validation, deferred work, and blockers. Review the diff and do not start the
   next phase.

### Responsive diagram remediation evidence

UG-65 reconciles and supersedes the 2026-07-18 responsive baseline. That
baseline counted 11 inline Mermaid diagrams and one semantic HTML/CSS Home flow,
but offline inspection found the built Mermaid hosts still depended on
Material's external `unpkg.com` fallback and did not render when HTTP(S) was
blocked. The release inventory is now eight source-backed Mermaid diagrams and
the Home flow. The Narrowband, Planetary, and Mosaic diagrams are excluded
because UG-61 through UG-64 defer those workflow claims.

Mermaid 11.16.0, its MIT license, and the AfterNight initializer are committed
under `docs/en/assets/javascripts/mermaid/`. The `an-mermaid` fence class avoids
the external Material path; strict local rendering, shared token-based colors,
responsive sizing, and accessible SVG titles/descriptions are enforced by
`build/scripts/validate_ug65_user_guide_diagrams.cjs`.

| Page | Diagram | Responsive/accessibility state |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Home (`docs/en/index.md`) | Typical deep-sky flow | Semantic ordered lists, accessible group label, minimum 13.60 px text, zero overflow. |
| Quick Start (`docs/en/getting-started/quick-start.md`) | Image Integration Lite state model | Source-backed state loop, title/description, minimum 12.49 px text, zero overflow. |
| Supported Formats (`docs/en/getting-started/supported-formats.md`) | File-format decision flow | Concise source-backed branches, title/description, minimum 13.15 px text, zero overflow. |
| Interface Overview (`docs/en/interface/overview.md`) | Dashboard/workspace state model | Only actual dashboard/workspace transitions, title/description, minimum 16 px text, zero overflow. |
| Process Catalog (`docs/en/processes/index.md`) | Deep-sky processing map | Typical rather than universal route, title/description, minimum 16 px text, zero overflow. |
| Image Integration Lite (`docs/en/processes/stacking-integration/image-integration-lite.md`) | Algorithm flow | Source-backed registration branch: standard groups write aligned lights, while drizzle groups retain transforms and resample during stacking; title/description, minimum 12.48 px text, zero overflow or clipped content. |
| Image List Analyzer (`docs/en/processes/stacking-integration/image-list-analyzer.md`) | Data flow | Exact scan/filter/sort/inspect/curate/export stages, title/description, minimum 13.61 px text, zero overflow. |
| Image Integration (`docs/en/processes/stacking-integration/image-integration.md`) | Conceptual pipeline | Source-backed registration branch: only non-drizzle groups write aligned lights and drizzle resampling occurs during stacking; title/description, minimum 12.33 px text, zero overflow or clipped content. |
| Broadband Imaging (`docs/en/workflows/broadband-imaging.md`) | Workflow decision flow | Verified acquisition-branch checkpoint and finish, title/description, minimum 16 px text, zero overflow. |
| Narrowband, Planetary, and Mosaic | Deferred-workflow diagrams | Excluded from release diagram evidence; each page states the evidence/re-entry boundary and the validator asserts no built Mermaid host. |

The 72-case offline browser matrix covers every retained diagram at 360×800,
390×844, 768×1024, and 1280×900 in light and dark modes. All cases pass with
zero horizontal overflow, zero clipped-content cases, and zero external Mermaid
requests. The validator enforces computed contrast thresholds for Mermaid nodes,
edges, and borders plus the semantic Home flow; the measured minima are recorded
with the exact source mappings,
hashes, commands, measurements, audit reconciliation, and the pre-existing
blocked MathJax-font limitation are recorded in
`docs/user-guide/audits/ug65-validation-evidence.md` and `.json`.

UG-16 mode-E execution record: the maintainer approved a private 2965×2967 32-bit floating-point M83 luminance master, SHA-256 `39285FD24E4E8660232EC11C5374C8978DABC92868A65ADA67F6ED3855EF7E77`. The run used Windows MSVC Release `AfterNight.exe`, SHA-256 `05BCA7B490E32E568170E14EB1F04F081EA1C5AAEDB094209148B3A5FE182E93`, at reviewed revision `51f1ae86286d0d2d19ae5ba923c93135d9aef611`; Internal Gaia first, Gaia DR3 G≤14, embedded WCS ignored, RA 204.25°, Dec −29.8667°, 1000 mm focal length, and 3.76 µm pixels. It detected 512 stars, retained 63 matches at 0.291 px RMS, solved in 490 ms at 0.772144 arcsec/px, and reported center RA 204.2544978°, Dec −29.8716965°. The private FITS was saved and reopened; the operator inspected fourth-order TAN-SIP WCS, solver tags, and retained acquisition/object fields. The output file was not committed, its private path is intentionally omitted, and a distinct post-save hash/raw console transcript was not retained. Consequently, the save/reopen details are a bounded manual observation in this checked-in execution record; the input/executable identities and focused automated results are the reproducible evidence, and no broader backend or distortion-quality claim is made.
